# Diffraction module knowledge pack

- Module: `hydrodynamics/diffraction`
- Repository: `digitalmodel`
- Path: `src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/docs/agent-pack.md`
- Pack revision: 3
- Code revision: `d921eedae7f527996d601c0c06959c5537fbc284`
- Analysis family: diffraction (AQWA and OrcaWave)
- Quality manager role: diffraction quality manager
- Status: DRAFT

The code revision is the citation pin; requirements below distinguish AQG r0 criteria from implementation defaults. This document specifies checks and does not authorize a solve or establish a gate clearance.

## 1. Purpose and scope

The main path converts canonical geometry, inertia and environment inputs to diffraction preparation workflows, then represents motion RAOs, added mass, damping and optional hydrostatics for export. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/input_schemas.py:L127-L215]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/output_schemas.py:L372-L391]`

The preparation examples select `convert_spec` for AQWA and OrcaWave; the solve example selects `run_orcawave` and requires a licensed interface. `[examples/workflows/aqwa-diffraction-deck-prep/input.yml:L1-L8]` `[examples/workflows/orcawave-input-prep/input.yml:L1-L8]` `[examples/workflows/orcawave-diffraction-solve/input.yml:L1-L15]`

The parent hydrodynamics routing row declares `production`; this is registry metadata, not demonstrated diffraction qualification. The README production and completeness statements remain README claims, unverified (U4). `[docs/registry/module-routing.yaml:L117-L122]` `[docs/domains/diffraction/README.md:L3-L21]`

The cylinder reference applies to a vertical circular cylinder with positive radius and draft below water depth; it computes heave radiation, rather than all six motion responses. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/analytical_cylinder.py:L15-L21]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/analytical_cylinder.py:L73-L99]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/analytical_cylinder.py:L288-L318]`

## 2. Inputs and outputs

Input contracts, including defaults, follow the canonical schema; a supplied value is not evidence that its physical basis has been checked. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/input_schemas.py:L127-L164]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/input_schemas.py:L251-L280]`

| Input                        | Unit / allowed domain                                                                                          | Default / upstream source                                                                                                                                                            | Evidence                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| Mass / inertia               | mass > 0 kg; CoG [x,y,z] m; gyration radii m or tensor kg·m²                                                   | explicit; tensor origin body_origin; free_floating requires gyration radii                                                                                                           | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/input_schemas.py:L167-L248]`                                                                                                                                                       |

| Frequency / period grid      | finite values > 0; angular frequency rad/s or period s; linear or logarithmic range with positive count        | frequency input; exactly one values/range; period conversion ω=2π/T, no sorting here                                                                                                 | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/input_schemas.py:L345-L411]`                                                                                                                                                       |
| Heading grid                 | degrees; explicit list or start/end/increment; positive increment                                              | range defaults 0 to 180 in 15-degree increments; symmetry false                                                                                                                      | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/input_schemas.py:L414-L463]`                                                                                                                                                       |
| Irregular-frequency removal  | none / interior_panels / control_surface                                                                       | interior_panels; legacy boolean excluded from serialization; `DiffractionSpec._validate_control_surface_method` rejects control_surface unless every body resolves a control surface | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/input_schemas.py:L466-L477]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/input_schemas.py:L523-L539]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/input_schemas.py:L1011-L1029]` |

The DOF order is surge, sway, heave, roll, pitch, yaw: translations along X/Y/Z and rotations about X/Y/Z. The geometry reference point defaults to [0,0,0] m. UNVERIFIED: end-to-end handedness, heading-zero direction, positive rotation and preservation of the same origin across readers and writers (U3). `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/output_schemas.py:L17-L24]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/input_schemas.py:L144-L164]`

AQWA motion phases are negated by `_build_rao_set`; the code describes conversion from lead `A cos(ωt+φ)` to lag `A cos(ωt−φ)`. This records implementation, not an independently checked standard clause. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/aqwa_converter.py:L266-L282]`

| Hydrostatics      | volume m³; mass kg; centres m; waterplane m²                                                                | optional result; stiffness is a 6×6 array                                                                     | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/output_schemas.py:L336-L356]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Excitation        | AH1 stores magnitude and phase degrees; magnitude normalization and lead/lag reference UNVERIFIED (U3)      | [body, heading, frequency, 6]; not a field of the unified export result                                       | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/aqwa_ah1_parser.py:L63-L104]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/output_schemas.py:L372-L391]`                                                                                                                                                                |
| Mean drift / QTF  | unit, wave-amplitude normalization, origin and phase convention UNVERIFIED (U3)                             | solve options name mean_drift, diagonal_qtf and full_qtf; unified export result has no such coefficient field | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/input_schemas.py:L509-L520]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/output_schemas.py:L372-L391]`                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Export files      | YAML references CSV; RAO CSV writes stored phases; added-mass/damping CSV carry per-term labels             | CSV, workbook and summary paths are returned by export_all                                                    | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/orcaflex_exporter.py:L49-L69]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/orcaflex_exporter.py:L80-L100]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/orcaflex_exporter.py:L145-L159]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/orcaflex_exporter.py:L182-L196]` |

The spreadsheet bridge copies supplied motion amplitudes/phases and labels rotations deg/m. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/orcawave_to_orcaflex.py:L115-L125]` Supplied coefficients use caller-supplied provenance, default `unknown`; absent coefficients yield zero 6×6 added-mass and damping matrices tagged `source="placeholder"`, rather than measured zero coefficients. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/orcawave_to_orcaflex.py:L67-L99]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/orcawave_to_orcaflex.py:L143-L170]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/orcawave_to_orcaflex.py:L196-L215]`

Named conversion helpers implement kg↔tonnes, density and inertia scaling, Hz↔rad/s, period↔rad/s, and radians↔degrees; complex phase is `degrees(angle(value))`. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/diffraction_units.py:L21-L56]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/diffraction_units.py:L63-L106]`

## 3. Method and governing standards

Tiered acceptance is implemented by `category_for`, `convergence_from_self_study` and `evaluate`; thresholds are engineering policies with rationales in code, not limits attributed to a licensed standard. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/cross_solver_acceptance.py:L84-L138]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/cross_solver_acceptance.py:L156-L187]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/cross_solver_acceptance.py:L205-L240]`

| Quantity          | Difference limit (%) | Denominator and coded basis                                               | Evidence                                                                            |
| ----------------- | -------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Hydrostatics      | 0.5                  | reference program term; same-mesh closed-form integral                    | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/cross_solver_acceptance.py:L84-L90]`   |
| Excitation        | 5.0                  | mode peak over solved frequency grid; well-conditioned diffraction result | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/cross_solver_acceptance.py:L82-L96]`   |
| Added mass        | 5.0                  | mode peak; well-conditioned radiation result                              | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/cross_solver_acceptance.py:L82-L96]`   |
| Radiation damping | 10.0                 | mode peak; mesh and formulation sensitivity                               | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/cross_solver_acceptance.py:L97-L101]`  |
| Motion RAO        | 5.0                  | mode peak; excitation, added mass and stiffness basis                     | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/cross_solver_acceptance.py:L102-L106]` |
| Resonant roll RAO | 10.0                 | mode peak; damping-sensitive potential-flow response, not a design value  | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/cross_solver_acceptance.py:L107-L112]` |

The category router assigns every roll motion-RAO request to the resonant-roll tier; it does not locate resonance. Each own-mesh residual must be ≤ 0.25 × its tier for comparable convergence. Inside-band differences without that evidence return PASS_CONDITIONAL; outside-band differences return FAIL. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/cross_solver_acceptance.py:L125-L138]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/cross_solver_acceptance.py:L149-L187]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/cross_solver_acceptance.py:L216-L240]`

Abscissa alignment requires increasing source axes, coverage and gap screening; signed matrix interpolation preserves signs and propagates non-finite coefficient columns. The nested numeric policy validates its domains before checking the justification. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/benchmark_abscissa.py:L62-L102]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/benchmark_abscissa.py:L157-L180]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/benchmark_matrix_alignment.py:L45-L80]`

The cylinder implementation records Yeung, “Added mass and damping of a vertical cylinder in finite-depth waters”, Applied Ocean Research 3(3), 1981, as its matched-eigenfunction source. Its potential convention is Re(φ exp(−iωt)), with unit heave velocity. The paper itself was not opened; independent source confirmation is U4. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/analytical_cylinder.py:L15-L21]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/analytical_cylinder.py:L49-L51]`

The equilibrium screens use caller-supplied tolerances, mass versus ρ∇, horizontal CoG–CoB separation, and signed component force balance; they do not establish GM or perform implicit unit conversion. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/equilibrium_checks.py:L1-L7]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/equilibrium_checks.py:L31-L91]`

AQG r0 §8 supplies the required diffraction gate criteria in section 5. Its panel-size guidance names DNV-RP-C205, Environmental conditions and environmental loads, with edition and clause still to be confirmed; no DNV tabulated value is reproduced here. UNVERIFIED: licensed clause editions, text and calculation Citation-sidecar coverage for the main path (U4). Clause text not checked against the licensed copy. (AQG r0, §8, G4.4.)

Private benchmark reference: dataset ID `diffraction-matched-benchmark`, revision r2 only; no private geometry, results or acceptance counts are reproduced. Evidence readiness remains U5, linked to the recorded incident. [digitalmodel#2140](https://github.com/vamseeachanta/digitalmodel/issues/2140).

## 4. Validated worked examples

“Validated basis” below means an independent closed-form expected value; regression pins remain separate. All G4 differences and partial implementations are tracked together in U1. A mesh WARNING may pass packaging, and a majority score can hide an individual failed check; it is not an AQG G4 PASS. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/quality_gates.py:L6-L14]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/quality_gates.py:L219-L242]`

## 8. Ownership

Required verifier: diffraction quality manager on a different provider from the producer. Required deputy role: deputy diffraction quality manager. Escalate missing inputs, conflicting tolerances, ambiguous standards and failed gates to the family quality manager; cross-family criteria require the maintainer’s disposition. These are role requirements, not a claim of a named appointment. (AQG r0, §11.)

| Downstream: OrcaFlex hydrodynamic handoff  | YAML/CSV/workbook export; native semantic proof is U3                                                                | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/orcaflex_exporter.py:L49-L69]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/orcaflex_exporter.py:L80-L100]`                         |

| Solver versions                            | version readiness requires native G1 evidence; none obtained; code has explicit API availability and execution paths | `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/orcawave_runner.py:L491-L514]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/aqwa_runner.py:L508-L537]`                              |

Downstream: BEMRosetta `OrcaFlexConverter.convert` calls `_call_orcaflex_exporter`, which constructs this module’s `OrcaFlexExporter` and calls its vessel-type, RAO, added-mass and damping writers; this is a caller/wrapper, not an independent validation route. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/bemrosetta/converters/to_orcaflex.py:L99-L105]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/bemrosetta/converters/to_orcaflex.py:L123-L144]`

The optional deck hydrostatic validator calls `run_bem_analysis`, including compute_hydrostatics=True; it is not a text-only validator and was not executed. `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/diffraction/deck_validation.py:L45-L70]`

Other implementations found by a repository-wide method search, including `scripts/`, require their own input and convention checks before comparison: `extract_aqwa_hydrostatic` reads AH1 mass/stiffness and derives GM from K44/K55 and mass; `compute_hydrostatics` clips a mesh and calculates hydrostatic integrals; hull-library `_hydrostatics` integrates signed panel volumes and waterplane moments. These are additional routes, not independent validation of this module. `[scripts/benchmark/compare_hydro_properties.py:L244-L281]` `[src/digitalmodel/naval_architecture/mesh_hydrostatics.py:L94-L125]` `[src/digitalmodel/hydrodynamics/hull_library/column_pontoon_form.py:L152-L168]`

The separate `create_hydrostatic_restoring` builds heave/roll/pitch stiffness from volume, waterplane area and GM, with g=9.81 m/s² by default; it also requires explicit common ρ/g under C4.1b and is not nominated as an independent hand-check reference. `[src/digitalmodel/orcawave/hydro_coefficients.py:L239-L265]`

Repository-wide implementation inventory: the five families below are selected because the map includes this module. The published map is pinned at `b5acbe5f61ed4c755c1f7ea4fafa4611bd14e407` and records searched code revision `947fa50ff9a9e210de7e8f90e01b37add85c5954`; each source locator below is reopened at this pack’s unchanged code pin. [digitalmodel, Repository implementation map, family index](https://github.com/vamseeachanta/digitalmodel/blob/b5acbe5f61ed4c755c1f7ea4fafa4611bd14e407/docs/registry/implementation-map.md). Inventory observations from this revision comparison: 440 + 295 + 31 + 137 + 17 = 920 map records; 920 − 5 absent records = 915 present source records. Absent records are retained as absence reports and are not counted as verified implementations. Source declarations/docstrings identify candidates; they do not establish numerical agreement, independence or validation. “not checked” includes unit, origin and phase agreement and the controls in section 5, tracked by U7. “none” means no covering pack is established at the code pin outside this module. Follow-up: the map lacks both marine coefficient-parser files, `hydrodynamics/aqwa/aqwa_lis_files.py`, the native AQWA vessel-type import script and the setting-audit script. Their pinned implementations are cited in the rows above; map extension and convention qualification are assigned to U7. The implementation inventory also omits `input_schemas.py` · `DiffractionSpec._validate_control_surface_method` (retained among the map's rejected input-validation candidates) and `report_computations.py` · `compute_stability`, both in `hydrodynamics/diffraction`; their rows are above. [digitalmodel, Repository implementation map, implementation inventory and rejected candidates](https://github.com/vamseeachanta/digitalmodel/blob/b5acbe5f61ed4c755c1f7ea4fafa4611bd14e407/docs/registry/implementation-map.yaml). Helper/caller symbols outside the map implementation rows are also named in section 3: `OrcaWaveDataExtractor.extract_rao_for_dof`, `_parse_force_rao`, `extract_report_data_from_owr`, `_build_appendices_html`, `build_input_comparison_html` and `_check_basis`; they require coverage links rather than claims of independent implementations. The four earlier hydrostatic routes plus `compute_stability` remain additional comparisons outside the five mapped families.

## 10. Coverage and open items

Reviewed coverage is the cited main path: canonical schemas and units; acceptance tiers and abscissa/matrix alignment; mesh/equilibrium/deck checks; LIS/AH1 parsing; runner and spreadsheet/export boundaries; selected benchmark orchestration, examples and diffraction documentation.
